## 06.8-01-PLAN.md

Path: `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-01-PLAN.md`

### Summary

Plan 01 changes one assignment in the normalizer and removes one branch in the menu leaf, then updates the tests and the five affected goldens. The cycle 3 delta is the A6 addition in task 01-01: a scoped non-strict case with no icon, an empty full caption and a nonempty short caption, with its deliberately chosen raw outcome.

### Strengths

- **A6 mechanism matches source.** `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:154-169` forces icon mode on an empty full caption, and the non-strict missing-icon branch only switches to label mode when `label` is nonempty. With the planned `cv_title = short_label` at line 202, the plan's expected output (short title, location marker, empty hidden span) follows directly. The stock leaf at `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/action.html:7-8` renders the guarded title and `{% cv_icon cv_icon %}`, and `render_icon(None)` returns an empty SafeString (`src/crud_views/lib/icons.py:231-234`), so "no visible icon or text" is the real outcome.
- **Existing analog test exists.** `tests/test1/test_button_data.py:293-298` already pins the both-empty, no-icon, non-strict case; the plan's new case is a one-field variant of it and uses the same `override_settings(CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=False)` pattern used throughout that module.
- **Golden scope is exact.** Only `list_full`, `list_disabled`, `toolbar_detail_full`, `toolbar_detail_disabled` and `toolbar_publisher_list` contain `data-cv-tooltip="context"` or `"list"`; the three card goldens carry only card markers, and cards set `short_label = label` (`button_data.py:133-137`), so their titles are unchanged. The five test names in 01-03 match `tests/test1/test_action_menus_golden.py:106-131`.
- **Old expectations are enumerated.** `tests/test1/test_action_menus_context.py:53`, `tests/test1/test_button_leaves.py:536`, `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py:16-24` (title equality, H1) and the stale comment at `tests/test1/test_context_action_label_mode.py:180` are all owned by a task.
- **Tracer expectation is correct.** The stock short caption for Update is the bare translated "Update" (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/snippets/action_short/update.html`), and the full caption includes the object (`snippets/action/update.html`).

### Concerns

None.

### Suggestions

- **Optional:** task 01-01 places "data-and-stock-leaf cases" in `tests/test1/test_button_data.py`, while leaf rendering helpers currently live in `tests/test1/test_button_leaves.py`. Module placement is planning latitude; either file satisfies the acceptance criteria.

### Risk Assessment

Low. Two small production changes with exhaustive existing coverage; the A6 case is a Python-only edge with a deliberately recorded outcome and no browser impact.

## 06.8-02-PLAN.md

Path: `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-02-PLAN.md`

### Summary

Plan 02 publishes executable action, menu and raw-context recipes, corrects the three caption rows and the context-button flowchart, and pairs the Breaking entry with a migration anchor. The cycle 3 delta is A9: links to `#action-tooltips` from both existing migration scan paths, with section-scoped test assertions.

### Strengths

- **A9 targets exist as described.** The "Tooltip markup" bullet sits in the "CSS and tests that select on markup" section (`docs/migrating-1.0.md:350`, bullet at 376-378) and the markup checklist item sits under "Silent breaks" (`docs/migrating-1.0.md:1458`, item at 1475-1478). Both are checklists a migrating reader actually scans, so the links are navigational and not duplicated recipes.
- **Section-scoped assertions have a tested precedent.** `tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py:108-118` already slices the migration text between markers before asserting, and `tests/test1/test_label_mode_docs.py` slices rows and sections by heading. The plan's requirement that "a link elsewhere in the file does not satisfy either checklist" is implementable with the same approach.
- **Changelog anchor convention is consistent.** Every Unreleased Breaking entry links as `docs/migrating-1.0.md#<slug>` (`CHANGELOG.md:14-66`), and `test_cancel_docs.py:109-111` shows the split-on-`### Breaking` assertion the plan reuses.
- **The rows and flowchart really contradict the new contract.** `docs/reference/buttons.md:423-426` says icon mode uses the full label as hidden text and title; `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:343` says the same in the RICON node; `docs/reference/action_menus.md:276` documents the context-menu title exception. All three are owned by task 02-01.
- **Historical example is handled, not deleted.** `tests/test1/test_button_leaves.py:194-240` and `docs/reference/buttons.md:398-402` keep the 0.30 menu copy for icon/label helper compatibility; the plan labels its titles unsupported rather than removing the helper (D-09 boundary).

### Concerns

None.

### Suggestions

- **Informational:** the anchor `#action-tooltips` requires the new heading to be literally "Action tooltips" (MkDocs slug). The plan names the anchor but not the heading text; the docs tests and strict build will catch a mismatch, so no plan change is required.

### Risk Assessment

Low. Documentation and tests only; the raw-context recipe delegates through `cv_button`, which normalizes permitted context (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:272-278`), so no new authorization path is introduced.

## 06.8-03-PLAN.md

Path: `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-03-PLAN.md`

### Summary

Plan 03 builds the tooltip-specific fixture server, Playwright runner and runner tests, then records browser JSON and the acceptance report. The cycle 3 delta covers A7 (explicit fixture-scoped TEMPLATES with `APP_DIRS=False` and preserved DIRS; a real contact row trigger registered before modal acceptance) and A8 (six retained screenshots under the phase directory with relative paths, hashes and acceptance links).

### Strengths

- **A7 loader instructions match Django's constraint and the local analogs.** `tests/browser/presentation_server.py:52-83` sets `APP_DIRS = False` before installing explicit loaders, and `tests/test1/test_form_button_docs.py:184-192` deep-copies `settings.TEMPLATES`, sets `APP_DIRS=False` and prepends a locmem loader. The inherited DIRS value the plan preserves is exactly `tests/test1/od/templates` (`tests/test1/conftest.py:86`), and the context processors at `conftest.py:89-95` are the ones the plan says to inherit.
- **Request-scoped loader pattern is real.** `presentation_server.py:170-191` sets the ContextVar token per request, renders deferred responses inside scope and resets in `finally`, which is what 03-01 prescribes.
- **A7 contact trigger is now executable.** Default row actions are `detail/update/delete` (`src/crud_views/lib/settings.py:83`); `tests/browser/cancel_navigation_server.py:99` is the precedent for `AuthorModalListView.cv_list_actions = ["detail", "delete", "contact"]`; `cv_list_actions_inline` exists on `CrudView` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:98`). `AuthorModalContactView` is a modal custom form with required `subject`/`body` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:908-932`), so the real 422 replacement path (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:90-95`) is reachable, and its `CustomFormView` base is object-bound (`src/crud_views/lib/views/form.py:10`), so `cv_context_button` finds `view.object` on GET and POST (`mixins.py:40`, `templatetags/crud_views.py:275-276`).
- **Modal lifecycle seams are present.** `src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js/tooltip.js:40-56` initializes on `cv:modal:loaded` and disposes on `cv:modal:unload`; `modal.js:10-24` dispatches both. `adapters.js:77-82` exposes `init/hide/dispose` as plain methods, so pass-through observers are feasible without touching production JS. `AuthorModalDetailView` has `cv_modal = True` (`views.py:893-895`), so the plan's "detail action is itself a modal trigger" is accurate.
- **A8 retention is committable.** Root `.gitignore` has no PNG rule; the only PNG exclusion is `.planning/ui-reviews/.gitignore`, which does not cover `.planning/phases/`. Relative paths from `06.8-browser.json` to `screenshots/*.png` are portable, and the evidence file is written last in the analog runner (`tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs:344-369`), so "validate after cleanup" is consistent with that flow.
- **Runner reuse is honest.** Only `loadAssets`, `assetFor`, `options`, `stopOwned`, `runtime` are exported (`cancel-navigation.cjs:372`); the plan imports four and reimplements the rest, and the source-bound `PYTHONPATH` pattern is at `cancel-navigation.cjs:92-93`.

### Concerns

- **LOW (optional, not a blocker):** the six screenshots are named in the 03-02 action and in `must_haves.artifacts`, but not in `files_modified`. The plan does say "retain the files with the phase evidence commit". Whether the executor's commit protocol stages undeclared untracked binaries is defined in an external file I could not inspect. If the executor requires declared paths, the executable addition is one sentence in 03-02: stage `screenshots/*.png` explicitly alongside `06.8-browser.json` in the evidence commit. Already-covered in intent; flagged only because the staging mechanism is unverified here.

### Suggestions

- **Informational:** `override_settings(TEMPLATES=...)` entered once around `serve_forever()` satisfies "scope active until server cleanup"; Django resets template engines on that signal, so "before the first template compilation" is a safety margin rather than a separate step.
- **Informational:** screenshot hash checks are within-run only (recorded, then re-validated after cleanup). That is the right scope; cross-run PNG determinism should not be asserted.

### Risk Assessment

Medium. This plan carries the real infrastructure work and the only runtime dependencies (cached Playwright/Chromium/assets, unverifiable here). The plan already treats infrastructure failure as failed/unexecuted evidence and keeps global strict mode intact, which bounds the risk.

- **No contradiction between plans.** Plan 01 asserts the raw empty-full/nonempty-short output and plan 03 asserts the hydrated name; plan 02's "guarded `cv_title` needs no markup edit" statement is consistent with `action.html:7` and with plan 01 leaving the stock leaf untouched.
- **Locked decisions D-01 through D-11** are each traceable: D-01/D-02/D-03 to 01-01 and 03-02; D-04/D-11 to 01-01, 01-02, 01-03, 02-01 and 03-02; D-05/D-06 to the acceptance report wording in 03-02 and the tracer notes in 01-01; D-07/D-08 to 01-02, 02-01 and the menu scenarios in 03-02; D-09 to 02-01/02-02; D-10 to 03-01/03-02. No plan reopens settled scope.
- **TIP-01 through TIP-05** are each owned: TIP-01 and TIP-03 by plan 01 (plus browser slices in 03), TIP-02 by 01-02 and 03-02, TIP-04 by plan 02, TIP-05 by plan 03. The TIP-04 classifier flag remains a disclosed probe limitation, not a gap.
- **Verification commands** are source-bound; the three cached nox environments, the venv and `examples/bootstrap5/pytest.ini` exist in the checkout. The one floor deselection is unchanged.

## 06.8-01

**Plan Path:** `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-01-PLAN.md`

### Summary
Wave 1 (`06.8-01`) updates `cv_button_data` in `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py` so icon-mode action buttons receive `short_label` instead of `label` in `data["cv_title"]` while retaining `label` (the full caption) in `data["cv_label"]` for `span.visually-hidden` in `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/action.html`. It also removes the context-only `title="{{ cv_title }}"` branch from `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/menu_item.html`, updates affected unit/render/accessibility tests (including `A6` for non-strict missing-icon + empty-full + nonempty-short captions), and refreshes only the five context/list golden snapshots in `tests/test1/golden/action_menus/`.

### Strengths
- **Exact projection seam and ordering (`D-01`–`D-04`, `TIP-01`, `TIP-03`):** In `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:133-140`, `card` sets `short_label = label` (preserving `cv_label` → `cv_action_short_label` → `cv_action_label` → `cv_key` precedence), while `context`/`list` resolve `label = ctx.get("cv_action_label", "") or ""` and `short_label = ctx.get("cv_action_short_label") or ctx.get("cv_action_label") or ""`. Changing only `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:201-202` from `data["cv_title"] = label` to `data["cv_title"] = short_label` after effective-mode and missing-icon resolution (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:144-173`) satisfies `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-01-PLAN.md:131-135` without touching `_form_button_data` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:292-394`) or `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/action.html:7-8`.
- **Closure of `A6` against actual fallback control flow (`D-01`–`D-03`, `TIP-01`, `TIP-02`):** In `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:154-169`, an empty full `label` forces `label_mode = "icon"` (line 155), and when `ctx.get("cv_icon_action")` is falsy under `CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=False`, `if label:` (line 165) is false, so `button_data.py:168-169` logs `"%s has neither an icon nor a label and renders empty"` and keeps `label_mode = "icon"`. With `data["cv_title"] = short_label`, a nonempty `short_label` produces `cv_title == short_label`, `cv_label == ""`, raw `title` and `data-cv-tooltip="<location>"` via `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/action.html:7`, and an empty `<span class="visually-hidden"></span>` via line 8. `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-01-PLAN.md:123,153-161,166,171` pins this exact data-and-leaf behavior, warning preservation, and strict-mode exception (`tests/test1/test_button_data.py:249-260`).
- **Complete menu-title removal and stale-assertion coverage (`D-07`, `D-08`, `TIP-02`):** Removing line 7 (`{% if cv_location == "context" and cv_title %}title="{{ cv_title }}"{% endif %}`) from `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/menu_item.html:1-8` while keeping `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/menu_item_label.html:1` (`{{ cv_short_label }}`) directly addresses all existing tests that encoded the old contract: `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py:16-24` (`_assert_icon_action_named`), `tests/test1/test_button_leaves.py:187-192,532-537`, `tests/test1/test_action_menus_context.py:42-55`, `tests/test1/test_action_label_mode_render.py:153-162`, and `tests/test1/test_context_action_label_mode.py:179-188` (`.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-01-PLAN.md:136-140,186-196`).

### Concerns
None.

### Suggestions
- **Informational / already-covered:** In `tests/test1/test_button_data.py:258`, `test_icon_mode_without_icon_falls_back_to_label` already accepts `caplog`; the new `A6` test in `tests/test1/test_button_data.py` can similarly assert on `caplog` for `"has neither an icon nor a label and renders empty"` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:169`) as specified in `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-01-PLAN.md:157-160`.

## 06.8-02

**Plan Path:** `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-02-PLAN.md`

### Summary
Wave 2 (`06.8-02`) updates the reference documentation (`docs/reference/buttons.md`, `docs/reference/action_menus.md`, `docs/reference/context_buttons.md`, `docs/reference/javascript.md`), pairs the `CHANGELOG.md` `### Breaking` entry with `docs/migrating-1.0.md#action-tooltips`, links `#action-tooltips` from both the `### CSS and tests that select on markup` and `## Silent breaks` checklists (`A9`), and creates `tests/test1/test_action_tooltip_docs.py` to extract and execute the documented `action-leaf`, `menu-leaf`, and `raw-context` recipes.

### Strengths
- **Deterministic recipe extraction contract shared with Wave 3 (`D-09`, `D-10`, `TIP-04`):** `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-02-PLAN.md:75-89,110-120` defines a byte-exact fence extraction and SHA-256 contract (`<!-- tooltip-recipe: action-leaf|menu-leaf|raw-context -->` in `docs/reference/buttons.md`) modeled on `tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py:20-21`, embedding `data-tooltip-recipe` on every root tag branch (`<a>` and `<button>`) and distinguishing flat leaf context (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:207-221`) from raw `ContextButton` context (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:186-193`).
- **Coordinated reference table and diagram updates (`A2`, `D-01`–`D-04`, `D-07`):** `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-02-PLAN.md:121-130` updates `cv_label`, `cv_short_label`, and `cv_title` in `docs/reference/buttons.md:423-426`, the theme recipe prose in `docs/reference/buttons.md:760-767` (preserving the invariants asserted by `tests/test1/test_label_mode_docs.py:331-335`), the `RICON` flowchart node in `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:343` (preserving `MICON` asserted by `tests/test1/test_label_mode_docs.py:288-297`), and removes the context-menu `title` exception in `docs/reference/action_menus.md:275-277`.
- **Closure of `A9` with section-scoped checklist verification (`D-09`, `TIP-04`):** `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-02-PLAN.md:144,149-153,170,173,228` explicitly updates `### CSS and tests that select on markup` (`docs/migrating-1.0.md:350-387`, beside `- **Tooltip markup** (#202)` at lines 376–378) and `## Silent breaks` (`docs/migrating-1.0.md:1458-1528`, including lines 1475–1478) with links to `#action-tooltips`, and enforces section-scoped assertions in `tests/test1/test_action_tooltip_docs.py` (using the `_section` pattern from `tests/test1/test_label_mode_docs.py:35-45`) so a link elsewhere in `docs/migrating-1.0.md` cannot satisfy either checklist.

### Concerns
None.

### Suggestions
- **Informational (existing test invariant on `docs/migrating-1.0.md` section placement):** When adding the new `action-tooltips` section to `docs/migrating-1.0.md` in Task `02-02` (`.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-02-PLAN.md:147`), do not place it between `### Table pagination overrides` (`docs/migrating-1.0.md:1402`) and `## Silent breaks` (`docs/migrating-1.0.md:1458`). `tests/test1/test_pagination_docs.py:38-41` asserts `text.index(HEADING) + len(HEADING) + len(section) == text.index(SILENT_HEADING)`, requiring `### Table pagination overrides` to remain immediately before `## Silent breaks`. Placing `## Action tooltips` at the top (like `## Dynamic Cancel navigation` at `docs/migrating-1.0.md:8`), under `## Presentation contracts` (`docs/migrating-1.0.md:234-332`), or at the end (after `## Modal adapter completed-hide lifecycle` at `docs/migrating-1.0.md:1529`) preserves that invariant cleanly.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW.** Documentation and recipe-test changes are well-bounded and verified by `tests/test1/test_action_tooltip_docs.py`, `tests/test1/test_label_mode_docs.py`, `tests/test1/test_form_button_docs.py`, and `mkdocs build --strict`.

## 06.8-03

**Plan Path:** `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-03-PLAN.md`

### Summary
Wave 3 (`06.8-03`) implements the owned loopback fixture server (`tests/browser/action_tooltips_server.py`), Playwright/Chromium runner (`tests/browser/action-tooltips.cjs`), and fail-closed runner unit tests (`tests/browser/action-tooltips.test.cjs`), then executes the full stock and migrated-custom browser matrix, retains six durable PNG screenshots under `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/screenshots/` (`A8`), runs the broad multicore pytest/JS/Ruff preservation suites, and writes `06.8-browser.json` and `06.8-ACCEPTANCE.md`.

### Strengths
- **Closure of `A7` part 1 — isolated `TEMPLATES` loader configuration (`D-09`, `D-10`, `TIP-05`):** `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-03-PLAN.md:109-118` requires deep-copying `settings.TEMPLATES` inside the fixture's `override_settings` scope before first template compilation, setting `APP_DIRS=False` when configuring explicit `OPTIONS.loaders` (`NativeLoader` + in-memory modal wrapper loader ahead of `django.template.loaders.filesystem.Loader` and `django.template.loaders.app_directories.Loader`), and preserving inherited `DIRS` (`Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "od" / "templates"` in `tests/test1/conftest.py:86`) and `context_processors` (`tests/test1/conftest.py:89-96`). This matches `tests/test1/test_form_button_docs.py:184-192` and avoids both Django's `ImproperlyConfigured("APP_DIRS must not be set when loaders is defined")` and the `DIRS` overwrite in `tests/browser/presentation_server.py:51-52`.
- **Closure of `A7` part 2 — server-owned `contact` row trigger and modal reopen flow (`D-10`, `D-11`, `TIP-03`, `TIP-05`):** Because `AuthorModalListView` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:888-891`) inherits the default `CRUD_VIEWS_LIST_ACTIONS = ["detail", "update", "delete"]` (`src/crud_views/lib/settings.py:83`) and does not expose `AuthorModalContactView` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:921-933`) by default, `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-03-PLAN.md:194-201,206,213-215` patches `AuthorModalListView.cv_list_actions` and `cv_list_actions_inline` to `["detail", "delete", "contact"]` (matching `tests/browser/cancel_navigation_server.py:99`), verifies that `/author_modal/` renders `exactly one [cv-key="contact"][data-cv-modal="true"]` control before modal assertions, and reuses that trigger to reopen `contact` after clicking the modal `detail` action (`AuthorModalDetailView`, `cv_modal = True` at `tests/test1/app/views.py:895`) replaces the modal content.
- **Closure of `A8` — durable phase-owned screenshots and hash/link verification (`D-10`, `TIP-05`):** `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/06.8-03-PLAN.md:30,42-43,82-83,156,221-230,240,243,245,260` retains the six PNGs (`stock-tooltip.png`, `custom-tooltip.png`, `stock-menu.png`, `custom-menu.png`, `stock-modal.png`, `custom-modal.png`) in `.planning/phases/06.8-action-tooltips-short-captions-and-visible-label-suppression/screenshots/`, records relative paths (`screenshots/*.png`) and SHA-256 digests in `06.8-browser.json`, verifies their existence and hashes *after* temporary-directory cleanup (with negative unit tests in `tests/browser/action-tooltips.test.cjs`), and links them via portable relative Markdown links in `06.8-ACCEPTANCE.md`.

### Concerns
None.

### Suggestions
None.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW.** All fixture, template-loader, modal-trigger, lifecycle, and evidence-retention mechanics are grounded in existing repository patterns (`tests/browser/cancel_navigation_server.py`, `tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs`, `tests/browser/presentation_server.py`, `tests/test1/test_form_button_docs.py`) with fail-closed unit and acceptance gates.

### Requirement and locked-decision coverage

All three `verify plan-structure` calls returned `valid:true`, zero errors/warnings and task counts 3/2/2. Plans retain waves 1/2/3, with plan 02 depending on 01 and plan 03 depending on both. This is structural validation only, not execution evidence.

| Requirement / decision | Executable coverage in current plans |
|---|---|
| TIP-01; D-01/D-02 | 01-01 request tracer, short/full/fallback/empty tests with independent hidden names; 03-01/02 stock/custom raw and hydrated checks. A6 is a bounded Python edge. |
| TIP-02; D-03/D-07/D-08 | 01-01/02 effective-mode and menu suppression tests; 02-01 copied leaves; 03-02 mixed-mode/menu fixtures preserve short text, order and toggle behavior. |
| TIP-03; D-04/D-11 | 01-01/02/03 card/form/custom, escaping/lazy, permission/state, POST/modal and lifecycle gates; 02-01/02 copies preserve hooks; 03-02 uses real live instances and scoped strict settings. |
| TIP-04; D-09 | 02-01 exact-byte executable recipes; 02-02 paired Breaking/migration and both migration checklist links; 03 consumes those exact identities and hashes. |
| TIP-05; D-05/D-06/D-10 | 03-01/02 owned stock/custom browser cases and durable evidence; mixed-mode outcome remains honest, external application access is excluded, UAT/runtime limits explicit. |

The TIP-04 deterministic edge-classifier limitation remains acknowledged in plan 02's `spec_less_fallback`; it does not erase the explicit executable migration requirements. Structural coverage does not claim tests or browser cases have passed.

### Cross-artifact fact drift — cycle 3

- **Phase status:** the seam returned `lag`, STATE “Ready to plan” versus ROADMAP “Planned”, nonterminal ranks 0 and 1. This is not a finding.
- **Success criteria:** ROADMAP.md:836–839 matches current must-have truths and tasks on short/full separation, empty tooltip suppression, visible/menu suppression, honest investigation and preservation/migration. Added retained screenshots elaborate the existing acceptance contract.
- **Requirements:** ROADMAP.md:831 and REQUIREMENTS.md:185–189 name TIP-01–TIP-05; current plan requirement lists and the table above cover all five without invented IDs.
- **Defined terms/decisions:** CONTEXT D-01–D-11 agree with current tasks. The no-icon/empty-full case preserves D-03's effective-mode ordering; it is not a visible fallback. The short tooltip remains nonempty while the full hidden caption remains empty, as already dispositioned. Loader isolation, real contact trigger and evidence retention implement the locked fixture scope. No contradiction was found.

All four axes were examined; no absent artifact caused a skipped axis. Advisory fact drift contributes to neither convergence count.

### Execution coverage limits

This cycle ran no product tests, browser acceptance, dependency installation or implementation. Cached path existence does not prove runtime compatibility; external cached assets were not read. Human visual/assistive-technology UAT and the full supported Python/Django CI matrix remain future work. The review evaluates plan completeness and feasibility, not finished product behavior.

# Cross-AI Plan Review — Phase 06.8, cycle 2 (baseline 61844b9 vs 36c6954)

All claims below were checked against the checkout at 61844b9. No files were edited, no tests run, no subagents used. The local Bootstrap bundle in `~/.cache` is outside the permitted read scope, so the one Bootstrap-internal claim is marked as not re-verified here.

## 06.8-01

**Summary.** The plan correctly locates the two production edits and now owns every stale test the change breaks. The normalizer assigns the full caption at `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:201-202`, after effective-mode resolution at lines 144-173, with `short_label` already resolved at lines 134-140. Swapping the right-hand side to `short_label` preserves card precedence because the card branch sets `short_label = label` at line 137, and the form path at lines 292-394 is untouched. The menu exception is the single branch at `menu_item.html:7`, and `menu_item_label.html:1` always prints `cv_short_label`, so deleting the branch is the whole menu change. The prior-cycle findings H1, A1, A3 and A4 are closed by executable content, not just ledger text.

**Strengths.**
- **H1 closed.** `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py:16-24` asserts `hidden.text == el.get("title")`; callers at lines 28-37 and 41-50 loop over every toolbar and row control, line 54-70 covers the POST `up` button, line 77-93 the filter toggle. The plan names the file, requires explicit `expected_full`/`expected_title` per control, and runs the module in Wave 1. Per-key expectations are derivable from the snippet templates (for example `snippets/action/update.html:3` versus `snippets/action_short/update.html:3`, `action_short/list.html` "List", `action_short/manage.html` "Manage", `action_short/up.html` "Up"), so "independently derived" is achievable.
- **A1 closed and source-correct.** `button_data.py:154-155` forces icon mode on an empty full caption; with an icon present the missing-icon branch at 156-169 is skipped; `cv_label` stays `""` (line 184) and the title becomes the short caption. `action.html:7-8` then emits title plus marker around an empty hidden span. The plan's stated raw outcome is exactly what the source will do.
- **A3 closed at the right trust boundary.** `CrudView.render_snippet` returns `mark_safe(result.strip())` at `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:421-435`, and the default captions interpolate `{{ object }}` inside `blocktranslate` (`snippets/action/update.html:3`), where autoescaping already applies. Testing hostile values through `object.first_name` rather than literal template source is the correct specimen; `Author.__str__` is `first_name last_name` (`tests/test1/app/models.py:19-20`), so the payload reaches both captions.
- **A4 closed.** `tests/test1/test_context_action_label_mode.py:180` carries the stale "only its title follows the mode" comment; its assertion at line 187 (`title is None`) remains valid. Plan 01-02 owns the file.
- **Stale expectation inventory is complete.** `test_button_data.py:49` and `:161` are the two full-title data expectations the plan cites; `test_button_leaves.py:187-191` (context-only menu title), `:536` (default-floor `"Details"`), `test_action_label_mode_render.py:161` (folded delete keeps title) and `test_action_menus_context.py:53` (`# cv_action_label as title`) are all assigned to 01-02. I found no other test comparing hidden text to title; `test_button_classes_templates.py:88` is formset controls and unaffected.
- **Golden scope is exact.** Six of eight goldens contain `title=`; the sixth, `cards_author_full.html`, is unaffected because the card's `short_label == label`. No golden contains `dropdown-item`, so menu removal cannot touch them, as 01-03 states. The five test names in 01-03 match `test_action_menus_golden.py:106-131`.
- **MENU_ITEM_030 handling is sound.** `test_button_leaves.py:194-240` never asserts a title, so marking the template historical costs nothing.

**Concerns.**
- **LOW. One degenerate edge gains a tooltip and is not pinned.** Empty full caption, nonempty short caption, no icon, non-strict: `button_data.py:165-169` takes the `else` branch (warns "renders empty"), mode stays icon, and after the change `cv_title` becomes the nonempty short caption. Today that control emits no attributes; after the change it emits title and marker on a control with no icon and an empty hidden span. 01-01 only pins the "with a real icon" variant. The configuration already warns, so this is not a blocker, but the observable change should be chosen deliberately and tested.
- **LOW.** The existing `_icon_only` helpers in `test_action_label_mode_render.py:270-275` and `test_context_action_label_mode.py:271-272` use truthiness only, so they pass before and after. The plan strengthens one adjacency case; that is enough, but the acceptance could be misread as broader than it is.

**Suggestions.**
- Add the no-icon/empty-full/nonempty-short non-strict case to 01-01's data tests and state the chosen outcome in the normalizer comment.
- In 01-01, assert `data-cv-tooltip` alongside `title` in the a11y helper so the marker contract is pinned at request level, not only in the leaf test.

**Risk: LOW.** The edits are two lines, every known consumer is enumerated, and the prior HIGH is addressed by executable test changes in Wave 1.

## 06.8-02

**Summary.** The documentation plan now covers every file that states the old contract, and the recipe contract is concrete enough that plan 03 can consume it byte-for-byte. A2 and A5 are closed. The one real gap is a migration-text nuance for copies that emit `title` unconditionally.

**Strengths.**
- **A2 closed.** `docs/reference/buttons.md:423` says the full label is "hidden text and `title`" in icon mode and line 426 ties `cv_title` to that; `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:343` labels RICON "the full label is hidden text and the tooltip"; `docs/reference/action_menus.md:275-277` documents the context-menu title exception. All three files are in the plan with coordinated row updates.
- **Doc-test invariants are respected.** `tests/test1/test_label_mode_docs.py:288-304` pins the literal MICON node, the first paragraph below the diagram, and the `cv_label_mode` row anchors and phrases; 02-01 explicitly preserves MICON and the section invariants, and 02-02 reruns that module. `buttons.md:755-757` phrases checked by the same module are named as preserved.
- **A5 closed.** The recipe contract fixes marker, fence language, body extraction and hashing in a way that matches the existing extractor at `tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py:20-21`, adds exactly-one validation, and puts identity attributes inside the fences on the root tag. Both stock leaves have a single root tag, so one attribute per recipe suffices.
- **Raw-context recipe is correctly scoped.** `cv_button` at `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:206-221` calls `cv_resolve_action(context.flatten())`, which hides denied actions (`visibility.py:12-18`) and flags disabled ones; `_render_context_action` at lines 186-192 passes raw ctx to custom templates, so a raw copy must delegate. The plan says exactly that and tests hidden and disabled targets.
- **Paired-migration test pattern exists** at `test_cancel_docs.py:108-118`, and the Unreleased Breaking section is at `CHANGELOG.md:7` with the link convention the plan reuses.
- **Preservation references are right.** `card-list-view.md:63` stays true for cards, `icons.md:171` is a same-label example, `javascript.md:97` ("the tooltip text is the element's title") stays true, `settings.md:120` is generic. I found no further doc asserting the full-caption tooltip.

**Concerns.**
- **MEDIUM. Migration text understates one copy shape.** 02-02 says an action copy "already consuming guarded `cv_title`" needs no markup edit. A copy that emits `title="{{ cv_title }}"` unguarded breaks D-02 after the change (empty-full/nonempty-short now yields a title where it did not before, and both-empty must omit attributes). The migration should state the guard is required, with the exact guard line from `action.html:7`.
- **LOW.** The `spec_less_fallback` block is process bookkeeping with no executable effect; harmless but it adds reading cost.
- **LOW.** "Tests must assert observable rendered behavior, not merely search for implementation strings" sits next to a requirement to assert three table rows and the RICON node text. Those are string assertions by nature; the plan should say the docs assertions are deliberate contract pins, like `test_label_mode_docs.py`.

**Suggestions.**
- Add "copies emitting `title` unconditionally must adopt the guard" to the migration section and test it with a both-empty render of the action recipe.
- Have the recipe test also render the menu recipe with `cv_title` present in flat data (icon inline mode) to prove the copy ignores it, mirroring 01-02's leaf case.

**Risk: LOW to MEDIUM.** Documentation-only, well-anchored to existing executable-doc patterns; the only risk is an incomplete migration statement.

## 06.8-03

**Summary.** The browser plan now constructs its own scenarios instead of assuming fixtures, scopes strict mode per request, and binds custom templates to the exact documented recipes. H2, H3, A1 (hydrated half), A3 (browser half) and A5 (consumption half) are closed in plan content, and every mechanism the plan leans on exists in the harness. Residual risk is operational: the fixture server has several setup details the plan leaves to the executor.

**Strengths.**
- **H2 closed.** `modal/content.html:8` includes `view.cv_content_template`, a plain class attribute (`base.py:123`, `form.py:51`), so an in-memory wrapper is patchable. `{% cv_context_button %}` resolves the view's object (`crud_views.py:272-278`), and `AuthorModalDetailView` is `cv_modal=True` with distinct default captions. modal.js delegates clicks at document level (`modal.js:203-210`, trigger `[data-cv-modal='true']`), so a detail action inside the modal performs real modal navigation. `tooltip.js:40,55,60-68` initializes on `cv:modal:loaded`, disposes on `cv:modal:unload` and hides on click; `adapters.js:77-82` are plain methods, so pass-through observers are feasible.
- **H3 closed.** `is_strict()` reads settings at call time (`src/crud_views/lib/exceptions.py:32-36`); `tests/test1/conftest.py` sets `CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=True`; `presentation_server.py:179-186` demonstrates request-scoped `override_settings` with `finally` reset. The plan's "record actual strict value per result" is the right proof.
- **Template isolation pattern is real.** `presentation_server.py:53-87` uses a ContextVar-gated locmem loader ahead of uncached loaders with explicit `loaders`, which disables Django's cached loader, and resets the token in `finally` (line 188). All list, card, context and menu rendering reaches the leaves through `cv_button` (`tags/list_action.html:1`, `card_action.html:1`, `context_action.html:1`, `actions_menu_item.html:1`), so overriding the two leaf names covers every location.
- **Helper reuse is accurate.** `cancel-navigation.cjs:372` exports the four named functions; `cached` (line 69) and `startServer` (line 91) are not exported, matching the plan's "implement locally". Env variables and candidate paths at lines 117-120 are as described.
- **Disabled expectations match the documented constraint** at `docs/reference/inaccessible_actions.md:46-48`.
- **Escaping specimen has precedent.** `presentation_server.py:92-93` already seeds a hostile `first_name`.

**Concerns.**
- **MEDIUM. Fixture-server setup details not stated.** Specifying `loaders` requires `APP_DIRS=False` (`presentation_server.py:52`) or Django raises at startup, and the contact modal is only reachable if the server sets `AuthorModalListView.cv_list_actions` to include `contact` as `cancel_navigation_server.py:99` does. Both are one-liners the executor will hit immediately, but the plan's "open contact via the existing modal trigger" implies the trigger exists by default, which it does not.
- **MEDIUM. One assertion depends on Bootstrap internals.** The empty-full case requires `aria-label` equal to the short caption after hydration. That follows from Bootstrap 5.3.x `_fixTitle`, which sets `aria-label` only when the element has no text content. This is consistent with the versioned source cited in RESEARCH.md, but I could not re-read the cached bundle in this review. If it ever differs, acceptance fails for a non-package reason; the plan should classify this check as recorded Bootstrap evidence rather than a package-contract gate.
- **LOW. Evidence durability.** Screenshots go under `/tmp/cv-068-browser` with hashes recorded in the report, but end-of-phase human UAT needs the images to still exist. A `/tmp` path may be gone by then.
- **LOW.** `sourceHashes` in the cancel runner globs `tests/browser/cancel*`; the new runner must hash `tests/browser/action*` or its own files are absent from evidence.
- **LOW.** Phase gates run three full cached Django series plus the example suite; these are long but correctly labelled as final gates, and the Vitest/declared-range mismatch disclosure is retained.

**Suggestions.**
- State `APP_DIRS=False`, the `cv_list_actions` contact registration, and preserved `DIRS` in 03-01's action text.
- Downgrade the hydrated `aria-label` check to "record and compare" with a clear note that the accessible-name policy is Bootstrap's, as A1's disposition already intends.
- Persist screenshots under the phase directory or a gitignored `tests/browser/.evidence/` path and record paths there.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** Mechanisms verified, but this plan has the most new code and the only non-package dependency in its hard assertions.

I'll verify each plan claim against the repository source and prior review dispositions, then write a finding for every plan.

## 06.8-01

Cycle 2: **H1, A1 (raw), A3 (data), and A4 are closed** in this plan. No remaining change on those items.

The production edit is the right one-line projection change. `cv_button_data` still assigns the full caption at `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:201-202` after mode resolution at `:154-173`, and context/list short text is already resolved at `:139-140`. Switching only that icon-mode right-hand side to `short_label` keeps card precedence (`:133-137`, `short_label = label`), leaves `_form_button_data` alone, and leaves the stock action leaf’s guarded title plus full hidden span at `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/action.html:7-8`. Menu suppression is the existing context-only branch at `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/menu_item.html:7`.

**Strengths**
- H1 is executable, not a promise. `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py:16-23` still equates hidden text with `title`. The task requires independent `expected_full` and `expected_title`, forbids deriving one from the other, keeps the filter toggle at `"Filter"`/`"Filter"` (`tests/test1/golden/action_menus/toolbar_publisher_list.html`), and runs that module in Wave 1.
- A1 matches the current order. An empty full caption forces icon mode before the visible-label swap (`button_data.py:154-155`, `:170-173`), so `cv_label` stays empty while a nonempty short caption becomes the title. The task pins that edge for context and list, including a configured visible mode, and does not add a full-caption guard.
- A3 uses the real trust boundary. `render_snippet` autoescapes then `mark_safe`s (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:425-435`). Hostile characters go in an interpolated value such as `object.first_name` (`:994-995`), not in trusted `template_code`.
- A4 is the stale comment at `tests/test1/test_context_action_label_mode.py:180`; the assertion at `:187` already expects no title. The task keeps that assertion and corrects the comment.
- The five golden files are exactly the context/list snapshots that contain `data-cv-tooltip="context|list"`. `cards_author_full.html` is card-resolved and must stay byte-identical.

**Concerns**
- LOW: The context/list a11y callers loop heterogeneous controls (`test_template_a11y.py:34-37`, `:47-50`). The plan does not print the per-key map. Full captions use curly quotes in `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/snippets/action/detail.html:3`. A single shared expectation, or ASCII quotes, fails the owned module rather than shipping the old equality.
- LOW: “Two old full-title expectations” are `tests/test1/test_button_data.py:49` and `:161` (`"Show"` vs short `"Detail"`). The new title string is not written out; the owned `test_button_data.py` run catches a wrong update.

**Suggestions**
- Spell the a11y map from the short snippets (`Update`, `Detail`, `List`, `Delete`, `Manage`, `Up`) and the current golden full strings, including `“`/`”`.
- In the default-floor leaf test, change only `tests/test1/test_button_leaves.py:536` from `"Details"` to the fixture’s short `"Detail"` (`:519`); keep the hidden span at `:537`.

**Risk Assessment:** LOW. The mechanism, failing assertions, and preservation gates are source-bound. Residual risk is executor transcription, and the Wave 1 commands fail closed.

## 06.8-02

Cycle 2: **A2 and the documentation half of A5 are closed.** TIP-04 stays an unclassified edge probe; this review acknowledges that classifier limit. The plan does not dismiss it. It executes the migration below. No new product decision is needed.

**Strengths**
- A2 names the live contradictions: `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:343` (RICON: full label is the tooltip), `docs/reference/buttons.md:423-426` (icon `cv_label` is hidden text and `title`), and `docs/reference/action_menus.md:275-276` (context menu native title). The task updates RICON, all three leaf rows, and the theme-recipe prose, and tests them so the old sentence cannot survive.
- A5’s fence contract is exact: one HTML-comment marker, LF, a `django` fence, untrimmed body, SHA-256 of those bytes, and root `data-tooltip-recipe` on every tag branch. Stock leaves do not gain the attribute (`action.html`, `menu_item.html`). Copied leaves must not include the stock action/menu leaves; only the raw-context recipe delegates through `cv_button` (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:206-221`).
- The extraction pattern already exists at `tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py:20-21`. Card, icon, and settings docs stay valid: card icon title equals the resolved caption (`button_data.py:133-137`), and `docs/reference/card-list-view.md:63` does not need an edit.
- Breaking text is paired with `docs/migrating-1.0.md`, with no legacy tooltip path, matching D-09.

**Concerns**
- LOW: `docs/reference/javascript.md:102` already says menu items carry no marker. The native-title exception is only in `action_menus.md`. Leaving the filter-toggle sentence at `javascript.md:336-337` is correct; an executor who “updates tooltips” there would document a change this phase does not make.
- LOW: A copied multi-branch menu recipe can miss `data-tooltip-recipe` on a disabled/POST branch. The contract requires it on every root branch, and the test rejects a missing identity, so this fails in 02-01 rather than in the browser.

**Suggestions**
- Assert the RICON node text and the three table rows by extracted section, not by a loose substring that a changelog sentence could satisfy.
- Keep any migration excerpt byte-equal to the canonical fence body; do not reindent it in `migrating-1.0.md`.

**Risk Assessment:** LOW. The public-contract edits are enumerated against current docs, and recipe tests render normalized data instead of searching for implementation strings.

## 06.8-03

Cycle 2: **H2, H3, A1 (hydrated), A3 (browser), and the runtime half of A5 are closed** in executable plan text. This review did not launch a browser and did not read cached Playwright, Chromium, asset bytes, nox interpreters, or `/tmp`. Those paths are outside this verification. Absence is not claimed.

**Strengths**
- H2 no longer assumes `tests/test1/app/views.py` already contains the matrix. The plan builds it in the new server. The seams exist: `AuthorModalContactView` at `views.py:921-932` with required `subject`/`body`; 422 on invalid modal POST at `src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:88-96` and `src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js/modal.js:186-196`; modal body include at `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/modal/content.html:8`; `{% cv_context_button %}` at `templatetags/crud_views.py:272`; label-mode maps and inline lists at `base.py:99-111`; `cv_inaccessible_actions` at `base.py:97`. Lifecycle events are `cv:modal:loaded` / `cv:modal:unload` in `tooltip.js:40-55`.
- H3 matches call-time strictness. `is_strict()` reads `CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT` per call (`src/crud_views/lib/exceptions.py:32-36`). `pytest_configure` sets it True (`tests/test1/conftest.py:49`). The missing-icon raise is `button_data.py:156-164`, and the non-strict visible fallback is `:165-167`. Scoping `override_settings(CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=False)` to that one render, and asserting True before and after, is the correct fix. `cancel_navigation_server.py:13-15` already calls `pytest_configure()` before imports.
- A5’s loader analog is real: `tests/browser/presentation_server.py:53-87` gates locmem with a `ContextVar` ahead of filesystem/app loaders. The four reusable exports are exactly `loadAssets`, `assetFor`, `runtime`, and `stopOwned` (`tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs:372`); `options` is exported and correctly excluded.
- Disabled icons keep raw title/marker without a required shown tooltip. Bootstrap `pointer-events` on disabled buttons makes a hover requirement a false failure. The plan refuses that shortcut.
- Empty-full hydration is consistent with an empty `<i>` (`src/crud_views/lib/icons.py:228`) and no text between icon and span (`action.html:8`). A mismatch fails the browser case instead of being treated as passed.

**Concerns**
- LOW: `AuthorModalDetailView.cv_modal = True` (`views.py:895`), so the in-modal detail control is itself `data-cv-modal`. Activation replaces modal content (`modal.js:203-214`) rather than performing a full-page navigation. The hide listener still runs (`tooltip.js:60-67`). “Close/reopen contact” must use the list trigger after that replacement. The criteria are observable, but the sequence is easy to over-specify.
- LOW: `AuthorUpdateView.cv_icon_action = "update"` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/update.py:25`). A process-wide `None` patch makes later strict icon-update renders raise. The plan requires request-scoped restoration; skipping that fails ordinary cases, which is the intended gate.
- Coverage limit: cached Chromium, Playwright, `cancel-assets-06.7`, and the Django 4.2/5.2/6.0 interpreters were not opened here. The plan’s fail-closed asset and source checks are the right substitute, not evidence those runtimes exist.

**Suggestions**
- Record the contact-modal detail control as a modal trigger, and reopen contact from the list after replacement, so “modal navigation” is not implemented as `location.assign`.
- Restore `cv_icon_action` in `finally` even when the non-strict render raises, and assert `CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT` on the next ordinary response.

**Risk Assessment:** MEDIUM. The fixture design matches existing servers and the 422/tooltip lifecycle, but real hover, focus, and disposal evidence does not exist until 03-02 runs, and this review could not confirm the cached browser runtime.

## 06.8-01

### Summary
Plan `06.8-01` cleanly implements the core caption projection and overflow-menu tooltip suppression changes (`TIP-01`, `TIP-02`, `TIP-03`). It changes only the icon-mode `cv_title` assignment in `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:201-202` from `label` to the already-resolved `short_label` (after effective-mode and missing-icon resolution at lines `144-173`) and removes the `{% if cv_location == "context" and cv_title %}title="{{ cv_title }}"{% endif %}` branch from `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/menu_item.html:7`. All Round 1 findings assigned to this plan (`H1`, `A1`, `A3`, `A4`, stale comments/docstrings, `MENU_ITEM_030` historical framing, and `test_button_leaves.py:536`) are thoroughly addressed in the executable task instructions and verification gates.

### Strengths
- **Minimal, well-ordered projection change:** Keeping `data["cv_title"] = short_label` at `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:201-202` after lines `154-173` preserves the empty-full-label icon-mode forcing (`if not label: label_mode = "icon"` at line `154`), strict/non-strict missing-icon handling (`lines 156-169`), card caption precedence (`short_label = label` at `lines 133-137`), and the separate `_form_button_data` path (`lines 292-394`).
- **Complete closure of H1 (`test_template_a11y.py`):** Task `01-01` explicitly owns `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py:16-94`, updating `_assert_icon_action_named(el)` (which currently asserts `hidden.text_content().strip() == el.get("title")` at line `23`) to take independent `expected_full` and `expected_title` parameters across all context, list, POST (`up`), and filter-toggle (`Filter`) callers, and adds `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py` to `01-01`'s automated `<verify>` gate.
- **Exact specification of A1 and A3:** Task `01-01` pins the `cv_action_label=""` + `cv_action_short_label="Edit"` edge case (`cv_label == ""`, `cv_label_mode == "icon"`, `cv_title == "Edit"`, empty `<span class="visually-hidden"></span>`, and `data-cv-tooltip`) and properly tests escaping across the `CrudView.render_snippet` `mark_safe` boundary (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:421-435`) by placing untrusted payloads in interpolated context variables (`{{ object.first_name }}`) and plain/`gettext_lazy` inputs rather than trusted template literals.
- **Complete closure of A4 and existing menu assertions:** Task `01-02` updates `tests/test1/test_button_leaves.py:187-192,536`, `tests/test1/test_action_menus_context.py:53`, `tests/test1/test_action_label_mode_render.py:153-162`, and the obsolete comment in `tests/test1/test_context_action_label_mode.py:180`.
- **Tightly scoped golden updates:** Task `01-03` limits snapshot regeneration to the five affected context/list goldens under `tests/test1/golden/action_menus/` while keeping card and form goldens untouched.

### Concerns
- **[LOW] Intra-wave test update ordering for `test_button_leaves.py:536`:** `tests/test1/test_button_leaves.py:536` (`assert a.get("title") == "Details"` in `test_defaults_setting_reaches_button_leaf`, where `cv_action_label="Details"` and `cv_action_short_label="Detail"`) breaks as soon as `cv_button_data` changes in Task `01-01`, but its update is scheduled in Task `01-02`. Because `01-01`'s `<verify>` runs only `test_action_label_mode_render.py -k icon_tooltip_request_tracer`, `test_button_data.py`, and `test_template_a11y.py`, the task gate passes, and `01-02` immediately updates and runs `test_button_leaves.py`. However, running `test_button_leaves.py` between `01-01` and `01-02` will fail until `01-02` is applied.

### Suggestions
- Keep the execution of `01-01` and `01-02` sequential within Wave 1 as planned, and do not run `test_button_leaves.py` until `01-02` updates line `536`.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW** — The production diff is two lines across two files (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:202` and `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/menu_item.html:7`), and every affected unit, accessibility, render, menu, and golden test in the repository is accounted for.

- **Complete closure of H2 (server-owned mixed/overflow/disabled and modal fixtures):** Task `03-02` no longer assumes `tests/test1/app/views.py` already has mixed-mode, overflow, disabled, or tooltip-bearing modal views. Instead, it configures request-scoped scenarios in `tests/browser/action_tooltips_server.py` and wraps `AuthorModalContactView.cv_content_template` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/form.py:19`, rendered inside `#cv-modal` by `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/modal/content.html:8`) to include `crud_views/view_custom_form.content.html` plus a real icon-mode `{% cv_context_button "detail" %}` targeting `AuthorModalDetailView` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:893-895`). - **Complete closure of H3 (scoped non-strict override):** Task `03-02` keeps `pytest_configure()`'s global `CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=True` (`tests/test1/conftest.py:43-49`) intact and wraps only the dedicated missing-icon fallback specimen (`AuthorUpdateView.cv_icon_action=None`, mode `icon`) in `override_settings(CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=False)`, recording and asserting `strict === false` only on that request and `strict === true` on ordinary requests before and after.
- **Complete closure of A1, A3, and A5 in the browser harness:**
  - Exercises `NativeLoader` (`ContextVar` + `MemoryLoader` ahead of uncached loaders, following `tests/browser/presentation_server.py:53-87,172-188`) across `stock -> custom -> stock` and AJAX modal replacements, verifying `data-tooltip-recipe` root attributes and exact SHA-256 recipe hashes (`A5`).
  - Verifies both raw response markup and post-hydration Bootstrap state for `empty-short`, `both-empty`, and `empty-full/nonempty-short` (where Bootstrap populates `aria-label` from the short `title` because the hidden `<span>` is empty, `A1`).
  - Places hostile quotes/ampersands/tags/Unicode in an untrusted model/context attribute interpolated into caption templates rather than literal `template_code` (`A3`).
- **Accurate helper reuse and readiness checks:** Task `03-01` imports only the actual exports of `tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs:372` (`loadAssets`, `assetFor`, `runtime`, `stopOwned`) and explicitly checks `bootstrap.Tooltip.VERSION`, `cv.initTooltips`, `cv.adapters.tooltip`, and `tooltip.js` network loading (`src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js/tooltip.js:70-71`, `adapters.js:77-82`).

### Concerns
- **[LOW] Rendering `{% cv_context_button "detail" %}` inside `AuthorModalContactView` requires `object` in template context:** In `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:272-278`, `cv_context_button(context, key, obj=None)` resolves `obj = getattr(view, "object", None)` when `obj` is omitted, and `CustomFormView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/form.py:10`) subclasses `DetailView`. However, confirm in `action_tooltips_server.py` that on a `POST` returning `422` (`CrudViewProcessFormMixin`), `view.object` is set (or pass `object` explicitly as `{% cv_context_button "detail" object %}`) so the context button resolves identically on both initial modal `GET` (`200`) and invalid modal `POST` (`422`). Let's note that `{% cv_context_button "detail" object %}` or ensuring `self.object` is bound on `POST` avoids any `view {cls} requires object` error during the 422 render.

### Suggestions
- In Task `03-02`'s in-memory `cv_content_template` wrapper for `AuthorModalContactView`, render `{% cv_context_button "detail" object %}` (or verify `view.object` is populated on `POST` before `form_invalid` renders the 422 response) so the modal context action renders identically on both initial `GET` and `422` `POST` replacement.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW** — The browser server and runner design closely follow the proven `cancel_navigation_server.py`, `cancel-navigation.cjs`, and `presentation_server.py` patterns, and all Round 1 gaps in fixture coverage, strict-mode scoping, and modal tooltip lifecycle verification have been closed.

### Already incorporated or source-resolved observations

- **Guarded copied leaves:** Fable's unguarded-title migration suggestion is already represented by plan 02's truth that empty titles omit generated attributes, task 02-01's exact copied-leaf render tests including empty captions, and task 02-02's deliberately conditional “already consuming guarded cv_title” statement. The complete canonical recipe preserves `action.html:7`'s guard. No extra product/plan change is required to satisfy the stated concern.
- **Modal POST object context:** Gemini's caution is directly resolved by `src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:40–49`: `post()` assigns `self.object` before `get_context_data()` and before the invalid-form path. `CustomFormView` inherits that mixin (`form.py:10`) and `cv_context_button` reads `view.object` (`templatetags/crud_views.py:272–278`). Plan 03 already requires the real 422 path with nonzero controls. Adding an explicit `object` argument is optional, not a missing mechanism.
- **Bootstrap hydrated name:** Plan 01 explicitly says this is existing Bootstrap title-to-name behavior, and plan 03 pins validated Bootstrap assets and checks their version. The locally read bundle's `_fixTitle` matches the specified outcome; inability of Fable to access that external cache is a coverage limitation, not evidence the gate should be weakened. This is already an intentional executable outcome and not a newly invented package naming API.
- **A11y marker / menu supplied title:** The real request tracer requires the location marker, and plan 02's location/mode copied-menu matrix already covers normalized icon data carrying `cv_title` while rendering no generated attributes. Additional assertions in a particular helper would duplicate existing requirements.
- **Docs string assertions:** The plan says tests must not *merely* search implementation strings and separately requires deliberate row/RICON pins. Those can coexist with rendered recipe assertions; no contradiction.
- **Intermediate default-floor failure:** Gemini's task-order note is already explicitly disposed in plan 01's ledger and sequential 01-02 gate. It is not an unresolved action.
- **Hash helper / broad preservation scope:** Plan 03 already requires local hash/report functions, current source/runtime/template identity and evidence; it does not import the cancel-only `sourceHashes` function. Long multicore phase checks, Vitest disclosure, remaining human UAT and bounded lifecycle tests are explicit existing plan content/dispositions.

- **Grok transcription/sequence suggestions:** Its per-key quote/caption caution is already covered by plan 01's independently derived exact expectations and explicit default-floor update. Its row/RICON extraction, all-branch recipe identity and canonical-excerpt equality observations restate plan 02 requirements. Its modal navigation and finally-restoration suggestions restate plan 03's real modal navigation, request-scoped restoration and ordinary requests after failed renders. Grok explicitly concludes no remaining pre-execution change on those points.

### Current counts and HIGH consensus gate

**CYCLE_SUMMARY: current_high=0 current_actionable=4**

Three substantive reviewers ran, so the consensus gate is engaged. No new HIGH was raised; no HIGH is suppressed as single-reviewer/unconfirmed. H1–H3 have explicit independent reviewer sign-off and are excluded as fully resolved plan concerns. A1–A5 likewise close with review confirmation. A6–A9 remain actionable because the additional cases/assignments/retention/navigation requirements are not yet fully present or explicitly disposed in executable PLAN content. Advisory fact drift contributes to neither count. No convergence is claimed while these four follow-ups remain.

### Cross-artifact fact drift — cycle 2

- **Phase status:** `drift-guard phase-status --phase 06.8` returns `lag`: STATE “Ready to plan”, ROADMAP “Planned”. This nonterminal lifecycle lag is not a finding.
- **Success criteria:** ROADMAP.md:836–839 and all three current `must_haves.truths` agree on short/full separation, empty tooltip suppression, visible/menu suppression, honest investigation and preservation/migration. Empty full text remains empty; a nonempty short tooltip is not an “empty tooltip”. Additional browser/evidence details refine the same contract.
- **Requirements:** ROADMAP.md:831 defines TIP-01–TIP-05, matching current Plan 01:22, Plan 02:16 and Plan 03:14 and REQUIREMENTS.md:185–189.
- **Defined terms/decisions:** CONTEXT.md D-01–D-11 agree with current tasks on unchanged full hidden captions (including empty), short-caption fallback, application-owned attribute preservation, immediate migration, stock/custom acceptance and the unconfirmed separate visible-label defect. D-02 does not create a new naming API or promise an existing nonempty accessible name when the full caption is empty. Bootstrap's current `_fixTitle` in the cached 5.3.3 bundle (line 6) supplies `aria-label` only when no aria-label or nonempty textContent exists; the A1 plan states and tests that consequence.
- All four axes were examined. No absent artifact or skipped fact-drift axis. No contradictory pair found. Advisory drift contributes to neither convergence count.

### Execution coverage limits

This is a plan review; no product changes, test runs, browser acceptance, new runtime installation or human UAT are claimed. Cached path existence does not prove future runtime compatibility or browser assertions. The full supported CI matrix and assistive-technology checks remain outside this review. Plan 02's TIP-04 classifier limitation remains explicitly acknowledged; executable recipes and acceptance tasks cover that requirement. Current lane participation, grounding discount markers and plan coverage are reported separately above.

## 06.8-01

**Summary.** The plan's two production edits are correctly located and minimal. `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:201-202` assigns `data["cv_title"] = label`, where `label` is still the full caption in icon mode because the short substitution at lines 170-173 only runs when `label_mode != "icon"`. Switching the right-hand side to `short_label` gives context/list the short caption and leaves cards unchanged, since the card branch sets `short_label = label` at line 137. The menu exception is exactly `menu_item.html:7`. The golden inventory is exact: only `list_full`, `list_disabled`, `toolbar_detail_full`, `toolbar_detail_disabled` and `toolbar_publisher_list` carry context/list titles, and `cards_author_full.html` has `title="Delete"` on a card icon action whose value is already the short caption, so it stays byte-identical as claimed. The test inventory is complete for the four modules named.

**Strengths.**
- The research-cited stale expectations all exist and are the right ones: `tests/test1/test_button_data.py:49,161` (`"Show"` becomes `"Detail"`), `tests/test1/test_button_leaves.py:187-191` (context-only menu title), `:532-537` (default floor `"Details"` becomes `"Detail"`), `tests/test1/test_action_menus_context.py:53`, `tests/test1/test_action_label_mode_render.py:161` (folded delete keeps title). Nothing outside the plan's `files_modified` asserts a context/list title value: `test_card.py:126`, `test_card_label_mode_render.py:114,126,249` and `test_button_classes_templates.py:244` are card or direct-leaf inputs.
- Short captions are real translated msgids (`snippets/action_short/*.html`: Detail, Update, Delete, List, Create, Manage, Up, Down), so no `.po` work is needed and D-01's "no renaming" holds.
- Custom `ContextButton` labels already flow into both captions (`buttons.py:77-80`), so D-04 is preserved by the data path without extra code.
- The filter toggle sets only `cv_action_label` (`buttons.py:353`); `short_label` falls back to it at line 140, so `title="Filter"` in `toolbar_publisher_list.html` is unchanged, matching the plan's "preserve filter-toggle title".
- Card overflow menus do exist (`src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:27,101-104`, `tags/card.html:9-12`, request tests in `test_action_menus_card.py:51,98`), so the context/list/card menu parameterization is not vacuous.

**Concerns.**
- **MEDIUM — undecided edge: empty full caption with nonempty short caption.** Line 154-155 forces icon mode when `label` is empty. Today `cv_title` is then `""` and the leaf emits nothing (`action.html:7`). After the change `cv_title` becomes the nonempty short caption, so a title and `data-cv-tooltip` appear on a control whose hidden span is empty. Bootstrap's `_fixTitle` then sets `aria-label` from the title because `textContent` is empty, so the accessible name becomes the short caption. The plan's truth "empty full with nonempty short retains existing effective icon mode" pins the mode but not the title. Decide explicitly (`short_label if label else ""` keeps old output; plain `short_label` changes it) and assert the chosen output in `test_button_data.py`.
- **LOW — comment at `button_data.py:170-173`** says icon mode keeps the full label "as the hidden text and the title (#216)". Plan says update it; make sure the test module docstring in `test_action_label_mode_render.py:1` and `buttons.md:423` stay consistent (plan 02 owns the doc).
- **LOW — disabled icon buttons in `list_disabled.html`/`toolbar_detail_disabled.html`** keep title and marker. `docs/reference/inaccessible_actions.md:46-47` documents that Bootstrap's `pointer-events: none` prevents any tooltip there. Fine for plan 01, but plan 03 must not assert a shown tooltip on them.

**Suggestions.**
- Add one parametrized data test for the empty-full/nonempty-short case with the decided value, and one asserting `cv_label` is untouched by the change for context/list icon mode.
- In 01-02, the existing `test_menu_item_leaf_has_no_tooltip_marker` (`test_button_leaves.py:567-570`) already covers the marker; the new parameterization only needs to add `title` across `context|list|card` × `icon|both|label`.

**Risk: LOW.** Two one-line edits, exact golden scope, complete stale-test inventory. The only open point is the edge-case title value.

## 06.8-02

The plan allows "an explicit linked recipe"; prefer that over duplication, or make the equality test mandatory rather than optional.

The actual migration for a copied action leaf is "nothing, if you kept the guard"; say so plainly.

## 06.8-03

**Summary.** The harness pattern is real and reusable: `cancel-navigation.cjs:372` exports `loadAssets, assetFor, options, stopOwned, runtime`; the server pattern (`cancel_navigation_server.py:13-41,143-171`) and the negative orchestration tests (`cancel-navigation.test.cjs:85-168`) are sound analogs. The cached runtimes exist (`.nox/tests-3-12-django-5-2`, `-4-2`, `crispy_floor-3-12-django-6-0`). But the plan overstates what the test app already provides and under-cites the closest analog for custom-template injection.

**Strengths.**
- Current-source guard (`cancel_navigation_server.py:35`) and `PYTHONPATH: ${root}/src:${root}` (`cancel-navigation.cjs:93`) are exactly the evidence-integrity controls the plan names.
- The plan correctly warns that hydrated `title` absence proves nothing; Bootstrap moves it to `data-bs-original-title`. Because the hidden span is nonempty, Bootstrap does not set `aria-label`, so the full accessible name check is meaningful.
- Independent cleanup stages and fail-closed report writing (`cancel-navigation.cjs:345-370`) are the right contract to copy.

**Concerns.**
- **MEDIUM — 03-02 read_first claims `tests/test1/app/views.py` supplies mixed-mode fixtures.** It does not. The only label-mode configuration there is `CardAction(key="delete", label_mode="icon")` at `views.py:79`; there are no `cv_*_actions_inline` keys or label-mode maps. Modal and POST views exist, but overflow menus, adjacent icon/label/both toolbar and row buttons, missing-icon fallback and `"disable"` mode must all be configured in the new server (as `cancel_navigation_server.py:99-101` does by assigning class attributes). Budget for that.
- **MEDIUM — disabled icon buttons cannot show a tooltip.** `inaccessible_actions.md:46-47` documents `pointer-events: none`. The matrix says "preserve disabled/active state"; hovering a disabled control to assert a shown tooltip will fail or be flaky. Assert raw attributes only for disabled controls.
- **MEDIUM — scope.** Re-proving modal open/replace/reopen, instance disposal and POST submission is re-verifying unchanged JS (`tooltip.js:38-55`, `adapters.js:77-82`) already covered by `tests/js/tooltip.test.js` (eight cases) and prior phases. One modal reopen specimen with a short tooltip is enough for D-11; the full lifecycle re-run inflates a 28k-token plan around a two-line change.
- **LOW — non-exported helpers.** `cached`, `startServer`, `sourceHashes`, `declarations` are not exported from `cancel-navigation.cjs`; the plan's "cached candidate lookup" must be duplicated or exported (which would touch a file not in `files_modified`). Reusing `options()` also inherits the `/tmp/cv-cancel-browser.json` default and `CV_CANCEL_*` names for a tooltip runner.
- **LOW — closer analog missed.** `tests/browser/presentation_server.py:56-87` already implements a request-scoped `locmem` loader subclass installed ahead of filesystem loaders. That is precisely the "scoped template loader" 03-01 asks for; PATTERNS cites only the cancel server.
- **LOW — Vitest.** `node_modules/vitest/package.json` is 5.0.3 while `package.json` declares `^5.0.5`, and the branch includes PR #301. `npm test` in 01-03 and 03-02 will run the stale install; disclose as planned, or run `npm ci` as an explicit, separately approved step.

**Suggestions.**
- Replace the read_first sentence with the concrete attribute assignments the server must make, and add `cv_inaccessible_actions = "disable"` on one target so disabled rows exist.
- Trim the matrix to: stock and custom icon hover/focus with full accessible name; adjacent labelled control with no instance; one menu per location opened; one missing-icon fallback; one modal reopen. Keep the rest in Python.
- Name the runner's env variables generically or document that `CV_CANCEL_*` is shared.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** Infrastructure is proven, but fixture configuration is understated, the disabled-tooltip expectation is unachievable as written, and the matrix is larger than the change warrants.

## 06.8-01

The production change is the right seam: icon-mode `cv_title` is assigned after mode resolution, and card/form paths stay intact. The plan misses a request test that still requires title to equal the full hidden caption, so wave 1 can go green while the suite is already wrong.

**Strengths**
- `cv_button_data` already resolves `short_label` with full fallback at `button_data.py:139-140`, then sets `cv_title` only in icon mode at `button_data.py:201-202`. Switching that assignment to `short_label` implements D-01 without a new API. Cards set `short_label = label` at `button_data.py:133-137`, so card titles stay the resolved caption. Form buttons return earlier at `button_data.py:120-125`.
- Stock list Update is a real short/full split: `action_short/update.html` is `Update`; `action/update.html` is `Update {{ verbose_name_translate }} “{{ object }}”`. The tracer helpers already exist in `test_action_label_mode_render.py:30-49`.
- Menu titles are only the context branch at `menu_item.html:7`. Real menus reach that leaf via `actions_menu_item.html:1` → `cv_button`. The five named goldens are the only snapshots with `data-cv-tooltip="list|context"`. Filter titles come from `context_action_filter.html:7-8`, not this assignment. The toggle at `actions_menu_toggle.html:3-6` has no title.

**Concerns**
- **HIGH:** `test_template_a11y.py:16-23` and `:28-70` require icon-action `title` to equal the visually hidden text for context and list links and POST buttons. After the change, title becomes `Update` / `Detail` / `Up` while the hidden span stays the full caption. That file is not in `files_modified` or the wave-1 commands. Wave 3’s full pytest will fail, and “fix the a11y helper” could shorten the accessible name and violate D-02.
- **MEDIUM:** `data["cv_title"] = short_label` emits a title when the full caption is empty and the short caption is not (`button_data.py:154-155`, `:201-202`). Today that title is empty, so the template emits nothing (`action.html:7`). A nonempty title with an empty hidden span can become the accessible name. The plan says keep the old accessible-name policy and also use the short caption. Those disagree on this edge.
- **LOW:** `test_defaults_setting_reaches_button_leaf` at `test_button_leaves.py:532-537` will fail after 01-01 (`title == "Details"` while short is `"Detail"`) and is only repaired in 01-02. Sequential tasks cover it; the 01-01 gate does not.

**Suggestions**
- Add `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py` to 01-01. Keep hidden text equal to the full caption; stop requiring `title == hidden`. Leave the filter-toggle call at `:93` unchanged.
- State the empty-full/nonempty-short outcome: either omit `cv_title` when `label` is empty, or accept a new tooltip and say the accessible name may fall back to it.
- Point the stale comment at `button_data.py:171-172`, not only the assignment.

**Risk Assessment:** MEDIUM. The mechanism is sound; the unnamed a11y equality test is the execution hazard.

## 06.8-03

The browser plan reuses a real owned-server and asset loader, and it correctly refuses to treat a missing `title` after Bootstrap init as proof. The prescribed Django bootstrap cannot render the required missing-icon fallback.

**Concerns**
- **HIGH:** 03-01 requires `pytest_configure()` before Django imports. That sets `CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=True` at `tests/test1/conftest.py:49`. `is_strict()` reads that setting at call time (`exceptions.py:32-36`). Icon mode with no icon then raises at `button_data.py:156-164` instead of falling back to visible text. 03-02 still requires that fallback in both stock and custom modes. Copying `cancel_navigation_server.py:13-15` makes the case a 500 unless the plan adds an explicit `CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=False` override for that specimen only.
- **MEDIUM:** Custom acceptance can pass on stock leaves. `cv_button` looks up `crud_views/tags/button/action.html` by name at `templatetags/crud_views.py:195-221`, so a loader override works, but the plan also says the recipe text must be exact and independently marked. A wrapper that includes the stock leaf, or a marker injected outside the fence, fails that test. The marker has to live in the plan-02 fence.
- **LOW:** `views.py` has modal/POST views (`AuthorModalListView` at `:888-905`) but not standing mixed-mode maps. Those are test monkeypatches, as in `test_action_label_mode_render.py:117-118`. The server must set them itself.
- **LOW:** `cancel-navigation.cjs` exports `loadAssets`, `assetFor`, `runtime`, `stopOwned`, and `options` (`:372`), not `startServer`. Reimplementing startup is required; reusing `options()` is fine only while cases stay `smoke|all`.

**Strengths**
- Asset validation, absolute `PYTHONPATH`, loopback, and current-source refusal match `cancel_navigation_server.py:34-39` and `cancel-navigation.cjs:26-63,91-107`.
- Bootstrap moves `title` in `Tooltip.getOrCreateInstance` (`adapters.js:77-81`). Checking raw HTML, instances, and hover/focus is the right proof. `tooltip.js:27-40` re-inits on `cv:modal:loaded`, so a modal case can exercise the existing lifecycle without a JS change.
- The full-suite commands and the crispy-floor deselect match the declared exclusion. Examples do not snapshot action titles.

**Suggestions**
- Add one non-strict URL or middleware override for the missing-icon specimen, and assert the other cases still run strict.
- Require the documented action/menu recipes to emit a stable identity attribute, and fail custom mode if the response still matches the stock leaf.
- Parse fences with the same regex as plan 02, without importing `test_action_tooltip_docs.py` into the server.

**Risk Assessment:** MEDIUM. Acceptance design is right; strict-mode bootstrap blocks one required case, and custom-template identity is still unspecified.

## Cross-plan

Wave order is valid: 01 changes projection and the menu leaf, 02 publishes the copies 03 must load, 03 should not invent a second template. Card and form preservation holds if the only production edit is the icon-mode right-hand side plus deleting `menu_item.html:7`.
