## OpenCode Review (gemini-review)

## 06.8-01

### 1. Summary
Plan `06.8-01` targets the core Python projection and template leaf changes for issue #304: switching icon-mode `cv_title` in `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py` from the full `label` to the resolved `short_label` (with full-caption fallback), removing the `context`-only native `title` branch from `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/menu_item.html`, and updating unit/render tests plus the five affected action-menu golden files. The production code changes are minimal and surgical, and the plan accurately identifies the five golden files that contain context/list action markup. However, source inspection reveals an unaddressed test module (`tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py`) that asserts `hidden.text_content().strip() == el.get("title")` on context and list actions and will deterministically fail once `cv_title` uses `short_label`.

### 2. Strengths
- **Minimal, well-ordered projection change (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:133-203`):** Changing `data["cv_title"] = label` at `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:202` to `data["cv_title"] = short_label` after effective-mode and missing-icon resolution (`lines 144-169`) preserves:
  - Card caption precedence (`lines 133-137`, where `short_label = label`),
  - Empty-full-caption icon forcing (`lines 154-155`, `if not label: label_mode = "icon"`),
  - Non-strict missing-icon fallback (`lines 156-169`), and
  - Full hidden accessible text (`"cv_label": label` at `line 184`, rendered into `<span class="{% cv_button_class cv_location 'hidden-label' %}">{{ cv_label }}</span>` in `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/action.html:8`).
- **Complete separation from form buttons (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:120-125, 292-394`):** Because `location == "form"` dispatches to `_form_button_data(ctx)` at `line 125`, changing `line 202` leaves form button `cv_title` (`line 391`) and caller attribute handling (`lines 358-365`) untouched.
- **Exact golden file scoping (`tests/test1/test_action_menus_golden.py:106-156`):** Across all 34 files under `tests/test1/golden/`, only the five files listed in `files_modified` (`list_full.html`, `list_disabled.html`, `toolbar_detail_full.html`, `toolbar_detail_disabled.html`, `toolbar_publisher_list.html`) contain `data-cv-tooltip="context"` or `data-cv-tooltip="list"` action buttons. The card goldens (`cards_author_full.html`, `cards_author_disabled.html`, `cards_publisher_child.html`) only emit `title="Delete"` on the icon-only card delete button, which already equals its `short_label`.
- **Clean overflow menu suppression (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/menu_item.html:7`):** Removing `{% if cv_location == "context" and cv_title %}title="{{ cv_title }}"{% endif %}` at `line 7` while keeping `{% include "crud_views/tags/button/menu_item_label.html" %}` at `line 8` (`{{ cv_short_label }}`) cleanly satisfies D-07/D-08 without altering `LEAF_KEYS` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:23-45`).

### 3. Concerns
- **[HIGH] Missed failing test file `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py` not in `files_modified` or verification gates:**
  - In `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py:16-24`, `_assert_icon_action_named(el)` asserts:
    ```python
    (hidden,) = el.cssselect("span.visually-hidden")
    assert hidden.text_content().strip()
    assert hidden.text_content().strip() == el.get("title")
    ```
  - This helper is called by `test_context_actions_are_labelled_links_without_button_role` (`lines 28-37`, on `/author/{pk}/detail/`), `test_get_list_actions_are_labelled_links_without_button_role` (`lines 41-50`, on `/author/`), and `test_post_list_action_is_a_button_that_submits_its_form` (`lines 54-71`, on `/author/` for `up`).
  - Once `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:202` sets `data["cv_title"] = short_label`, `el.get("title")` on those views becomes `"List"`, `"Detail"`, `"Update"`, `"Delete"`, `"Manage"`, and `"Up"`, while `hidden.text_content().strip()` remains `"List of all Authors"`, `"Show Author “Douglas Adams” detail."`, `"Move Author up"`, etc.
  - All three tests in `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py` will fail at `line 23`. Because `test_template_a11y.py` is omitted from `06.8-01-PLAN.md` (`files_modified` and `<verify>` commands), this failure will go undetected in Wave 1 and Wave 2 and break the full-suite gate in Wave 3 (`06.8-03` task `03-02`), where `test_template_a11y.py` is also outside `files_modified`.
- **[MEDIUM] `render_snippet` `mark_safe` behavior on literal characters in `*_template_code` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:421-435`):**
  - Task `01-01` and Threat `T-06.8-01` call for exercising quote/markup/Unicode payloads through Django leaves to verify autoescaping.
  - Note how captions are produced in `CrudView.render_snippet` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:421-435`): `Template(template_code).render(TemplateContext(data))` autoescapes template variables (`{{ object }}`), and `line 435` wraps the result in `mark_safe(result.strip())`.
  - If a test passes hostile characters (`"`, `<`, `>`, `&`) as literal template source in `cv_action_short_label_template_code` or `ContextButton(label_template_code=...)` rather than via a template context variable (`{{ ... }}`) or a plain `str`/`Promise` in `cv_button_data(ctx)`, `Template.render` treats literal template text as trusted HTML and `mark_safe` prevents `action.html:7` (`title="{{ cv_title }}"`) from escaping literal quotes. Tests for autoescaping must distinguish plain strings / interpolated `{{ ... }}` variables (which Django autoescapes) from raw template literals in `*_template_code`.
- **[LOW] Stale comment in `tests/test1/test_context_action_label_mode.py:180`:**
  - `test_menu_folded_key_keeps_icon_and_short_label` (`tests/test1/test_context_action_label_mode.py:179-188`) passes (`assert item.get("title") is None` because the test sets target mode to `"label"`), but the comment at `line 180` (`# a menu item renders icon and short label in every mode; only its title follows the mode, as documented (#216)`) describes the removed pre-#304 behavior.

### 4. Suggestions
- Add `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py` to `06.8-01-PLAN.md` `files_modified` and update `_assert_icon_action_named` (`tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py:16-24`) so context/list actions assert both a nonempty full hidden label (`span.visually-hidden`) and the expected short `title` (while `test_filter_toggle_is_a_labelled_button` at `lines 78-93` continues to assert `Filter` on `#cv-filter-toggle`). Include `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py` in Task `01-01` or `01-03`'s `<verify>` pytest command.
- In Task `01-01`, test hostile quote/angle-bracket/ampersand payloads both via plain `str` / `gettext_lazy` inputs to `cv_button_data()` and via variable interpolation (`{{ object }}`) in view/button label templates.

### 5. Risk Assessment
- **Risk Level: MEDIUM**
- **Justification:** The production change itself is low risk (2 lines across `button_data.py` and `menu_item.html`), but omitting `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py` from `files_modified` and Wave 1 verification guarantees a broken full test suite in Wave 3 unless corrected in Plan `06.8-01`.

## 06.8-02

### 1. Summary
Plan `06.8-02` updates reference documentation (`docs/reference/buttons.md`, `docs/reference/action_menus.md`, `docs/reference/javascript.md`), migration guidance (`docs/migrating-1.0.md`), and `CHANGELOG.md`, and introduces `tests/test1/test_action_tooltip_docs.py` to extract and execute fenced action-leaf, menu-leaf, and raw-context recipes. The executable-fence pattern mirrors `tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py` (`lines 20-27`) and `tests/test1/test_form_button_docs.py` (`lines 180-193`) well. However, there is an unreferenced contradiction in `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:343` and a second stale cell in `docs/reference/buttons.md:423` that need to be addressed so documentation stays internally consistent.

### 2. Strengths
- **Executable documentation recipes (`tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py:20-27` analog):** Extracting `tooltip-recipe: action-leaf`, `tooltip-recipe: menu-leaf`, and `tooltip-recipe: raw-context` directly from `docs/reference/buttons.md` and rendering them against real normalized/raw contexts prevents documentation drift and provides single-source templates for Plan `06.8-03`.
- **Clear separation between raw context and flat leaf contract (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:93-100, 186-221`):** `ContextButton` templates (`template` / `template_code`) render via `_render_context_button` (`lines 93-100`) and `_render_context_action` (`lines 186-192`) with raw resolved action dicts (`cv_url`, `cv_action_label`, `cv_action_short_label`, `cv_icon_action`), whereas `tags/button/action.html` and `tags/button/menu_item.html` receive `cv_button_data()`'s flat `LEAF_KEYS` (`lines 207-221`). Explicitly documenting and testing `{% cv_button "context" %}` delegation for raw custom templates closes a common theme-author pitfall.
- **Paired Breaking / migration verification (`CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/migrating-1.0.md`):** Enforcing the `docs/migrating-1.0.md#action-tooltips` anchor in `CHANGELOG.md` and running `mkdocs build --strict` matches repository conventions (`CLAUDE.md`, `tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py:108-118`).

### 3. Concerns
- **[MEDIUM] `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:343` is omitted from `files_modified` but states the old tooltip rule:**
  - In `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:343`, the Mermaid flowchart under `## How a context button is rendered` states:
    ```mermaid
    OUT{"final mode"}
    OUT -- "icon" --> RICON(["icon only; the full label is<br/>hidden text and the tooltip"])
    ```
  - Additionally, `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:365-373` cross-references `cv_button_data()` and `crud_views/tags/button/action.html`.
  - Leaving `docs/reference/context_buttons.md` untouched while updating `docs/reference/buttons.md` and `docs/reference/action_menus.md` leaves a direct contradiction in the reference docs for context buttons. (Note: `tests/test1/test_label_mode_docs.py:288-298` inspects the `MICON` node and first paragraph of that section, not the `RICON` node text, so updating `RICON` is safe when keeping the existing `MICON` node and paragraph markers intact.)
- **[MEDIUM] `docs/reference/buttons.md:423` (`cv_label` row) also states `(hidden text and title)`, not just the `cv_title` row (`line 426`):**
  - Task `02-01` instructs: `"Correct the action cv_title table to state short/full fallback versus full hidden text; retain card/form distinctions."`
  - In `docs/reference/buttons.md:423-426` (`### The leaf contract`):
    - `line 423` (`cv_label`): `on context and list the full label in icon mode (hidden text and title) and the short label in label / both (the visible text)`
    - `line 424` (`cv_short_label`): `the short label, used for menu items`
    - `line 426` (`cv_title`): `the title text, which also marks the button with data-cv-tooltip="<cv_location>" for tooltip.js; present only when cv_label_mode is "icon"`
  - If only `line 426` (`cv_title`) is updated, `line 423` (`cv_label`) will still falsely claim that `cv_label` is both the hidden text and `title` on `context` and `list` in `icon` mode, and `line 424` (`cv_short_label`) will still say `used for menu items` only.
- **[LOW] Existing section-slicing assertions in `tests/test1/test_label_mode_docs.py:331-335`:**
  - `test_label_mode_docs.py:331` calls `_section(buttons, "## A theme recipe")` (`docs/reference/buttons.md:722-768`), which slices from `## A theme recipe` to the next `<= ##` heading (`## System checks` at `line 769`). Any edits or added recipes in `docs/reference/buttons.md` must preserve the required phrases checked at `test_label_mode_docs.py:332-334` (`'package card floor in CRUD_VIEWS_LABEL_MODE_DEFAULTS is "both"'`, `"is None unless a view sets it"`, `"cv_get_card_actions_label_mode()"`).

### 4. Suggestions
- Add `docs/reference/context_buttons.md` to `06.8-02-PLAN.md` `files_modified` and update node `RICON` at `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:343` (e.g., `OUT -- "icon" --> RICON(["icon only; full label is hidden text,<br/>short label is the tooltip"])`), while preserving the `MICON` node and prose invariants checked by `tests/test1/test_label_mode_docs.py:288-298`.
- In `docs/reference/buttons.md:423-426`, update all three rows (`cv_label`, `cv_short_label`, and `cv_title`) in `### The leaf contract` so `cv_label` describes the full hidden label in `icon` mode (without `and title`), `cv_short_label` notes its use for `cv_title` in `icon` mode and menu items, and `cv_title` describes the resolved short caption (falling back to full caption) on `context`/`list` and resolved card caption on `card`.
- Ensure the `tooltip-recipe: action-leaf` and `tooltip-recipe: menu-leaf` fences in `docs/reference/buttons.md` coordinate with Plan `06.8-03` on how custom-template rendering is identified in the DOM (e.g., whether the recipe itself includes a custom theme class/attribute or whether `action_tooltips_server.py` injects a marker attribute when loading the fence).

### 5. Risk Assessment
- **Risk Level: LOW**
- **Justification:** This plan is confined to documentation, changelog, and a new doc-recipe test module, with `test_label_mode_docs.py`, `test_form_button_docs.py`, and `mkdocs build --strict` catching structural or link regressions immediately.

## 06.8-03

### 1. Summary
Plan `06.8-03` builds a dedicated real-browser acceptance server (`tests/browser/action_tooltips_server.py`), Playwright runner (`tests/browser/action-tooltips.cjs`), and Node test suite (`tests/browser/action-tooltips.test.cjs`) modeled on `tests/browser/cancel_navigation_server.py` and `tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs`, then runs the full multi-environment verification gates and records `06.8-browser.json` and `06.8-ACCEPTANCE.md`. Reusing the validated offline asset loader and process-lifecycle helpers from `cancel-navigation.cjs` (`lines 26-84, 372`) is solid. However, several assumptions about what `tests/test1/app/views.py` and `cancel-navigation.cjs` provide out of the box need explicit handling in `action_tooltips_server.py` and `action-tooltips.cjs`.

### 2. Strengths
- **Direct reuse of hardened browser harness primitives (`tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs:26-84, 372`):** Importing `loadAssets`, `assetFor`, `runtime`, and `stopOwned` from `tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs` reuses SHA-256/SRI verification, path-containment checks, symlink-preserving Python runtime resolution, and bounded SIGTERM/SIGKILL child teardown without duplicating asset-loader tests.
- **Proper Bootstrap 5.3.3 tooltip verification strategy (`src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js/adapters.js:77-82`, `tooltip.js:20-68`):** Checking raw server HTML (`title` and `data-cv-tooltip`) alongside hydrated DOM (`bootstrap.Tooltip.getInstance(el)`, `data-bs-original-title`, hover/focus `.tooltip.show .tooltip-inner` text, and accessible name) avoids the pitfall where Bootstrap 5's `Tooltip` strips `title` on initialization.
- **Fail-closed orchestration and independent cleanup stages (`tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs:345-370`):** Testing browser close rejection, server teardown, and report writing in `tests/browser/action-tooltips.test.cjs` ensures a failed browser assertion never leaks an ephemeral `make_server` process or temporary SQLite directory.

### 3. Concerns
- **[HIGH] Existing modal views in `tests/test1/app/views.py:882-933` do not contain any `[data-cv-tooltip]` elements inside `#cv-modal` by default:**
  - Task `03-02` `<read_first>` states: `"Existing tests/test1/app/views.py supplies modal, POST, disabled/active and mixed-mode fixtures"`, and `<action>` requires: `"Exercise real link/POST/modal flows, including a tooltip-bearing control inside a supported modal, content replacement, reinitialization, activation hide and disposal of detached instances."`
  - Inspecting `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/modal/content.html:1-9` shows that modals render only `.modal-header` (title + `button[data-cv-modal-dismiss]`) and `.modal-body` (`{% include view.cv_content_template %}`); they do **not** render `{% cv_context_actions %}`.
  - Inspecting `tests/test1/app/views.py:888-933` (`AuthorModalDetailView`, `AuthorModalDeleteView`, `AuthorModalContactView`) shows:
    1. `AuthorModalDeleteView` and `AuthorModalContactView` render form buttons (`delete`/`submit` and `cancel`), which default to `default_label_mode("form") == "label"` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/label_mode.py:20`) and therefore emit **no** `title` or `data-cv-tooltip="form"`.
    2. `AuthorModalDetailView` uses the default `table-inline` object-detail layout with `["first_name", "last_name"]` (no `detail` property tooltip).
  - Consequently, if `action_tooltips_server.py` mounts `cv_author_modal` as-is from `tests/test1/app/views.py`, `#cv-modal` will contain **zero** `[data-cv-tooltip]` elements on `cv:modal:loaded` and `cv:modal:unload`!
  - Furthermore, form buttons in `"icon"` mode (`_form_button_data` in `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:390-391`) only have a single caption (`cv_title == cv_label`), so if the modal test intends to verify **short tooltip vs. full hidden accessible label** on an action leaf inside `#cv-modal`, `action_tooltips_server.py` must also include an action button (or configure `cv_form_actions_label_mode = {"submit": "icon", "cancel": "icon"}` on `AuthorModalContactView` for 422 replacement lifecycle plus an action control inside modal content).
- **[MEDIUM] `cancel-navigation.cjs` readiness and script-response checks do not verify `tooltip.js` or `bootstrap.Tooltip` (`tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs:182-183, 338`):**
  - `cancel-navigation.cjs:182-183` waits only for `bootstrap?.Modal.VERSION === '5.3.3'`, `cvGetConfig`, `cvModalOpen`, and `cv?.adapters?.modal`.
  - `cancel-navigation.cjs:338` asserts only `['viewset.js', 'modal.js', 'adapters.js']` in `report.responses`.
  - If `action-tooltips.cjs` copies those checks without adding `tooltip.js`, `globalThis.bootstrap?.Tooltip?.VERSION === '5.3.3'`, `typeof globalThis.cv?.initTooltips === 'function'`, and `!!globalThis.cv?.adapters?.tooltip`, it will not explicitly gate on the tooltip script and adapter loading.
- **[MEDIUM] Template loader caching and custom-leaf identity between `stock` and `custom` modes in `action_tooltips_server.py`:**
  - Task `03-01` requires `action_tooltips_server.py` to serve both `stock` and `custom` modes and prove that `custom` mode actually rendered the extracted documentation recipes (`tooltip-recipe: action-leaf` and `tooltip-recipe: menu-leaf`) rather than stock templates.
  - In `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:221`, `cv_button` calls `django.template.loader.render_to_string(_BUTTON_LEAVES[kind], ...)`, which resolves templates via `django.template.engines["django"]`.
  - If `action_tooltips_server.py` switches modes per request (e.g. via cookie or query/header), it must use `override_settings(TEMPLATES=...)` with `django.template.loaders.locmem.Loader` (which triggers Django's `setting_changed` signal and flushes the template engine cache on entry and exit, as in `tests/test1/test_form_button_docs.py:180-193`), and either stamp a deterministic attribute on the in-memory custom templates (after hashing the exact extracted recipe text from `docs/reference/buttons.md`) or use a recipe class/attribute already present in the documented fence.
- **[LOW] Quoted/Unicode custom short caption specimen in `action_tooltips_server.py` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:421-435`):**
  - As noted in `06.8-01`, `CrudView.render_snippet` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:435`) wraps `Template(template_code).render(...)` in `mark_safe(...)`. When creating the browser specimen for `TIP-05 encoding` ("A representative quoted/Unicode custom short caption appears literally in the real tooltip, with independently preserved full accessible text and no markup injection"), `action_tooltips_server.py` should supply the quoted/markup/Unicode string via a template context variable (e.g., `{{ object }}` on an `Author` instance whose name contains `"`, `&`, `<b>`, and Unicode) or a plain string / `gettext_lazy` return value, rather than putting unescaped literal quotes/tags directly inside `template_code`.

### 4. Suggestions
- In `tests/browser/action_tooltips_server.py`, explicitly configure a modal view that renders `[data-cv-tooltip]` triggers inside `#cv-modal` across 200 open and 422 form-replacement cycles (for example, setting `cv_form_actions_label_mode = {"submit": "icon", "cancel": "icon"}` on `AuthorModalContactView` and/or rendering an icon-mode action button inside the modal body template) so `cv:modal:loaded` initialization, click `hide()`, and `cv:modal:unload` `dispose()` are genuinely exercised on live modal tooltip instances.
- In `tests/browser/action-tooltips.cjs`, extend the `goto()` readiness check (`cancel-navigation.cjs:182-183`) to assert `globalThis.bootstrap?.Tooltip?.VERSION === '5.3.3' && typeof globalThis.cv?.initTooltips === 'function' && !!globalThis.cv?.adapters?.tooltip`, and include `'tooltip.js'` in the required package script list (`cancel-navigation.cjs:338`).
- In `tests/browser/action_tooltips_server.py`, hash the raw `tooltip-recipe: action-leaf` and `tooltip-recipe: menu-leaf` fence strings extracted from `docs/reference/buttons.md` before registering them in `locmem.Loader`, and wrap custom-mode requests in `override_settings(TEMPLATES=...)` so Django's template cache never serves stale stock templates in custom mode (or vice versa).

### 5. Risk Assessment
- **Risk Level: MEDIUM**
- **Justification:** Browser tests involving Bootstrap 5 tooltips, dropdowns, and 422 modal replacements have subtle lifecycle interactions, and `tests/test1/app/views.py` does not include tooltip-bearing controls inside `#cv-modal` without explicit fixture configuration in `action_tooltips_server.py`. Addressing the modal fixture configuration, template-loader cache scoping, and `test_template_a11y.py` update (from `06.8-01`) will make Wave 3 execution smooth and deterministic.

## Consensus Summary

### Agreed Strengths

- The two production edits are correctly located: the action normalizer currently assigns full `label` to `cv_title` after effective-mode resolution (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:202`), while the menu leaf's context-only native-title exception is at `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/menu_item.html:7`. Card precedence and the separate form path can stay intact.
- Full hidden text already renders independently of the title (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/action.html:7–8`). The five golden files are a bounded scope, executable documentation recipes have a real precedent, and plan ordering follows the production → documentation → browser dependency.
- Browser raw-HTML checks, actual tooltip instances, template identities, source/runtime hashes and independent cleanup are valuable safeguards. The existing browser harness exports the four functions named by plan 03 (`tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs:372`).

### Agreed Concerns

- The old full-title/hidden-label equality survives in `test_template_a11y.py:23` (Gemini/Grok), the empty-full/nonempty-short outcome is not pinned (Fable/Grok), and the leaf-contract table still states the old equality (Gemini/Grok). See H1, A1 and A2.
- Browser fixture setup is understated: `tests/test1/app/views.py:79` has only the card action mode override; it does not preconfigure the adjacent/mixed/overflow/disabled cases claimed by plan 03. Modal views at `tests/test1/app/views.py:882–933` also lack an action tooltip inside their body. All three reviewers flag fixture setup omissions at different severities. See H2 for the consolidated required plan change.

### Current unresolved findings and source confirmation

| ID | Severity / classification | Reviewers | Grounded finding | Required plan change |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | HIGH; existence-class, independently confirmed | Gemini, Grok | `_assert_icon_action_named` requires full hidden text to equal `title` at `tests/test1/test_template_a11y.py:16–24`; context/list/POST callers at lines 28–70 will violate that old invariant after the planned short-caption change. This file is absent from plan 01's edit/targeted verification inventory. | Add this file to plan 01; independently assert exact full hidden name and short tooltip for action controls, preserve the filter-toggle case, and include the module in Wave 1 verification. |
| H2 | HIGH; existence-class, independently confirmed; overlapping Fable MEDIUM | Gemini, Fable/Grok (fixture setup) | Plan 03's `read_first` claims existing mixed/disabled fixtures; source supplies modal/POST routes but no such configuration. `modal/content.html:1–9` renders no context toolbar; modal classes at `tests/test1/app/views.py:893–932` have ordinary property/form bodies, and the form floor is `label` at `src/crud_views/lib/view/label_mode.py:24`. Plan 03 requires a tooltip-bearing modal control and rejects zero specimens, but does not describe constructing the control or its replacement path. | Correct plan 03's fixture assumption and specify server-owned mode/menu/permission assignments plus a real icon action with distinct short/full text inside modal content through open, replacement and reopen. Require nonzero initialized instances before hide/disposal assertions. Cite `tests/browser/presentation_server.py:56–87` as the existing scoped-loader pattern. Disabled specimens should preserve raw attributes/state without requiring a shown tooltip. |
| H3 | HIGH; existence-class, independently confirmed | Grok | Plan 03 bootstraps with `pytest_configure()`, which sets `CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=True` at `tests/test1/conftest.py:49`; `is_strict()` reads that value dynamically (`src/crud_views/lib/exceptions.py:32–36`), and icon-without-icon raises before visible fallback (`button_data.py:156–166`). The required missing-icon browser specimen cannot run under that default. | Add a request/specimen-scoped `CRUD_VIEWS_STRICT=False` override for missing-icon fallback in plan 03, and assert other specimens retain strict mode. Use existing scoped override patterns; do not weaken the whole fixture server. |
| A1 | MEDIUM | Fable, Grok | Empty full plus nonempty short forces icon mode (`button_data.py:154–155`); the planned assignment produces a title beside an empty hidden span. The plan pins effective mode but leaves raw title/marker and post-Bootstrap accessible-name expectations implicit. | In plan 01, explicitly pin the selected title/marker and unchanged hidden-span outcome, and state the corresponding hydrated accessible-name expectation without claiming a new accessible-name policy. Test that precise edge rather than only the mode. |
| A2 | MEDIUM | Gemini, Grok | `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:343` explicitly calls the full caption the tooltip and is outside plan 02's file list. Adjacent `cv_label`/`cv_short_label` rows at `docs/reference/buttons.md:423–426` also need the coordinated contract. | Add context_buttons.md to plan 02 and update the icon flowchart; explicitly update all three leaf-contract rows while retaining card/form distinctions. |
| A3 | MEDIUM (also LOW browser manifestation) | Gemini | `CrudView.render_snippet` marks rendered template text safe at `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:421–435`. Literal hostile quotes in trusted template source are not the same input boundary as plain strings or interpolated context data, so a naively constructed fixture could test the wrong contract. | Specify plain/lazy-string and interpolated-context payloads for the Python and browser escaping specimens in plans 01/03; document that trusted template literals do not acquire a new escaping policy in this phase. |
| A4 | LOW | Gemini, Grok | `tests/test1/test_context_action_label_mode.py:180` says a menu item's title follows its mode, which becomes stale after the universal removal. The file is not an edit target. | Add the existing module/comment to plan 01's edit inventory and update the obsolete title-mode explanation while preserving its existing behavior assertion. |
| A5 | MEDIUM | Grok; Gemini related identity coordination suggestion | Plans 02/03 require exact extracted recipes and independently marked custom rendering but do not pin where the distinguishing markup lives. A marker outside the rendered leaf does not prove that the exact custom leaf, rather than a stock include, produced the control. | Coordinate plans 02/03 on stable identity attributes inside the documented action/menu fences, exact HTML-comment fence marker syntax/extraction, raw recipe hashes and DOM assertions that reject stock delegation. Preserve request-scoped loader/cache isolation. |

No HIGH is suppressed by the consensus gate: H1, H2 and H3 assert concrete source/configuration facts independently confirmed above. Judgment-class findings would retain weight from any unmarked source-cited reviewer even without corroboration; no leading evidence-discount marker is present in the completed reviews. Plan mitigation intentions do not constitute completed/sign-off closure of these newly raised HIGHs.

### Dispositions and divergent views

- Fable initially described the stale-test inventory as complete; Gemini identified `test_template_a11y.py`. Direct source resolves that disagreement in Gemini's favor (H1).
- Fable's recipe duplication MEDIUM is already incorporated in plan 02 task 02-02: use a linked recipe or executable equality checks for **any** duplicated snippets. It is not an unresolved action.
- Fable's stale normalizer comment is explicitly assigned in plan 01 task 01-01. Historical `MENU_ITEM_030` unsupported-output labeling is explicitly assigned in plan 01 task 01-02 and plan 02 task 02-01. Neither is an unplanned action.
- Disabled tooltip visibility is a real platform constraint (`docs/reference/inaccessible_actions.md:46–48`), but the plan never requires a disabled control to show a tooltip; its disabled requirements are preservation/state checks. The H2 fixture clarification records this boundary without inventing a product change.
- Fable's broader lifecycle scope objection does not change D-10/D-11 or plan 03's explicit lifecycle acceptance. Those checks are intentionally planned; no additional acceptance gate or external-app prerequisite is introduced.
- Fable's non-exported helper caution does not invalidate the named imports: plan 03 only imports `loadAssets`, `assetFor`, `runtime`, `stopOwned`, all exported. It says to implement its own `--case`/`--output` and copy necessary owned-server/cached lookup patterns; it does not promise exported `cached`, `startServer`, `sourceHashes` or inherited `options()`.
- Gemini's script-readiness suggestion is covered at the observable level by required actual hover/focus tooltip assertions and missing-result rejection. `tooltip.js` absence cannot pass those gates. The executor may use the suggested readiness check as an implementation detail.
- Template cache isolation is already required by plan 03’s scoped loader and non-vacuous stock/custom checks. A5 narrows the remaining coordination issue to exactly where the recipe-owned identity marker lives and how both phases extract/hash it; the existing scoped loader analogue is included in H2.
- Grok’s observation that the default-floor leaf test changes in task 01-02 is already covered by that sequential task and its verification; it is not an unplanned repair. Its non-exported startup helper observation agrees with the explicit copy-necessary-pattern instruction, not a new import requirement.
- Existing label-mode doc invariants, raw denied/disabled hooks and Vitest/runtime disclosure are explicitly covered by plan 02/03 verification and preservation actions. Fable's other documentation locations were inspected: card descriptions retain their resolved-caption behavior; generic same-label examples do not contradict the change.

### Convergence counts

Current unresolved HIGHs: **3** (H1–H3). Current actionable non-HIGHs: **5** (A1–A5). These count unique plan changes, merging duplicate Python/browser escaping observations and overlapping fixture observations. Advisory cross-artifact drift is excluded. No execution results are claimed.

## Verification coverage

Effective authority: `intel`, resolved with `drift-guard authority --raw` (automatic upgrade from grep because intel.enabled=true). `.planning/intel/API-SURFACE.md:5` declares the index empty and `.planning/intel/api-map.json` is absent. - **Success criteria:** compared `.planning/ROADMAP.md:836–839` with all three plans’ `must_haves.truths`. - **Authoritative terms/decisions:** CONTEXT.md D-01–D-11 compared with all plan objectives/actions/truths. “Short caption”, “full hidden accessible caption”, visible/menu automatic-tooltip suppression, preserved application-owned attributes, immediate migration and stock/custom browser scope agree. Parent-reported checks: locked-decision coverage 11/11; requirements coverage 5/5; all 20 automated commands have `fails_when`. `verify-command-paths` returned `not_applicable` for every command, not verified.
